#### `std::vector< SimilarityResult > findSimilarAdapters(const std::string &domain, const std::string &base_model_id, std::size_t k) const`
- Source: `include/tensor/adapter_repository.h`:266
- Brief: Find the top-k adapters most similar to the given adapter.
- Parameters:
  - `domain` (const std::string &): Domain tag of the query adapter.
  - `base_model_id` (const std::string &): Base model identifier of the query adapter.
  - `k` (std::size_t): Maximum number of results to return.
- Return: Sorted list (descending cosine score) of similar adapters. Empty when the graph is not set, the adapter is not registered, or k == 0.
- Details: Delegates to the wired TensorFingerprintGraph. By default, key-based similarity uses TT cosine (inner-product based). If setExactSimilarityFn() is set, that backend overrides scoring. // STUB/SIMULATION NOTE (STUB #177): // Purpose: Expose adapter similarity search backed by fingerprint graph. // Activation: Only when setFingerprintGraph() has been called. // Production Delta: Uses exact TT cosine by default and supports // optional backend override via setExactSimilarityFn(); does not yet // include ANN acceleration/indexing for large candidate pools. // Removal Plan: Q3 2027 — replace with full TTTrain::innerProduct() // similarity + HNSW index over fingerprints (Phase 4 AdaLoRA bridge). domain Domain tag of the query adapter. base_model_id Base model identifier of the query adapter. k Maximum number of results to return. Sorted list (descending cosine score) of similar adapters. Empty when the graph is not set, the adapter is not registered, or k == 0.

#### `storage::TTTrain toTTTrain() const`
- Source: `include/tensor/ht_train.h`:150
- Brief: Flatten the HT representation to a TT-train (memoized).
- Parameters: none
- Details: On the first call, reconstructs the full dense tensor and re-decomposes it as a TT-train using TensorTrainDecomposer. The result is cached behind a mutex so subsequent calls return the cached value without recomputing. Intended for compatibility with ITensorIndex; not efficient for large tensors on the initial call. Cache is invalidated when the HTTrain is move-assigned. Stub #286 resolved: memoization behind tt_cache_mtx_ / tt_cache_ eliminates repeated O(∏ n_k) reconstruction cost. The long-term removal plan (Q2 2028) is to extend ITensorIndex to support IHierarchicalTuckerIndex directly, removing the round-trip entirely.

### themis::tensor::HissReshaper

#### `void clearQuanticsFn()`
- Source: `include/tensor/hiss_structural_search.h`:205
- Brief: Remove the custom quantics backend; the built-in path is used again. Thread-safe.
- Parameters: none
- Details: Clear Quantics Fn. Calls: lk().

#### `QTTrain exposeQuantics(const storage::TTTrain &train, const std::vector< std::size_t > &grid_sizes)`
- Source: `include/tensor/hiss_structural_search.h`:242
- Brief: Reinterprets a TT train in a pure-binary quantics-friendly layout.
- Parameters:
  - `train` (const storage::TTTrain &): Input TT train. Must be non-empty.
  - `grid_sizes` (const std::vector< std::size_t > &): Physical dimension sizes for the reshaped view. If empty, train.mode_sizes is used verbatim. Must have the same length as train.mode_sizes and the same element product when non-empty.
- Return: QTTrain in the pure-binary quantics layout.
- Throws:
  - std::invalid_argument: if train is empty, grid_sizes length mismatches train.mode_sizes, or element products differ.
- Details: Reconstructs the dense tensor, pads each physical dimension to the next power-of-two extent with trailing zeros (in flat C-contiguous element order), and decomposes the padded tensor into an all-2 quantics-mode train. The returned QTTrain carries: grid_sizes / padded_grid_sizes — original and padded extents, bit_depths — binary depth per dimension (log₂(padded_grid_sizes[d])), original_element_count — number of valid (non-padding) elements, mapping — QTTMappingDescriptor for lossless physical↔QTT index conversion; use it to distinguish valid payload from zero-padding when round-tripping back to the original dense tensor. Dimensions that are already a power of two are not padded further (grid_sizes[d] == padded_grid_sizes[d]). When a QuanticsFn backend is installed via setQuanticsFn(), the backend is called instead of the built-in path; in that case mapping is the responsibility of the custom backend. train Input TT train. Must be non-empty. grid_sizes Physical dimension sizes for the reshaped view. If empty, train.mode_sizes is used verbatim. Must have the same length as train.mode_sizes and the same element product when non-empty. QTTrain in the pure-binary quantics layout. std::invalid_argument if train is empty, grid_sizes length mismatches train.mode_sizes, or element products differ.

### themis::tensor::QTTMappingDescriptor

#### `std::size_t physicalToQTT(std::size_t physical_idx) const`
- Source: `include/tensor/hiss_structural_search.h`:127
- Brief: Convert a flat physical index to a flat QTT index.
- Parameters:
  - `physical_idx` (std::size_t): Flat index into the unpadded tensor. Must satisfy physical_idx < product(grid_sizes).
- Return: Flat QTT index into the quantics-mode tensor.
- Throws:
  - std::out_of_range: if physical_idx >= product(grid_sizes).
  - std::invalid_argument: if the descriptor is empty or inconsistent.
- Details: Interprets physical_idx as a C-contiguous (row-major) flat index into a tensor of shape grid_sizes, extracts the per-dimension indices, and encodes each dimension's index into bit_depths[d] binary QTT modes (MSB first), yielding a flat C-contiguous index into the all-2 quantics-mode tensor. physical_idx Flat index into the unpadded tensor. Must satisfy physical_idx < product(grid_sizes). Flat QTT index into the quantics-mode tensor. std::out_of_range if physical_idx >= product(grid_sizes). std::invalid_argument if the descriptor is empty or inconsistent.

#### `std::optional< std::size_t > qttToPhysical(std::size_t qtt_idx) const`
- Source: `include/tensor/hiss_structural_search.h`:145
- Brief: Convert a flat QTT index to a flat physical index.
- Parameters:
  - `qtt_idx` (std::size_t): Flat index into the quantics-mode tensor. Must satisfy qtt_idx < product(padded_grid_sizes).
- Return: The corresponding flat physical index in the unpadded tensor, or std::nullopt when qtt_idx addresses a zero-padded element (any reconstructed per-dimension index ≥ grid_sizes[d]).
- Throws:
  - std::out_of_range: if qtt_idx >= product(padded_grid_sizes).
  - std::invalid_argument: if the descriptor is empty or inconsistent.
- Details: Interprets qtt_idx as a C-contiguous (row-major) flat index into the all-2 quantics-mode tensor, reconstructs the per-dimension indices from the packed bit sequence (MSB first per dimension), and maps them back to a flat physical index in the original unpadded tensor. qtt_idx Flat index into the quantics-mode tensor. Must satisfy qtt_idx < product(padded_grid_sizes). The corresponding flat physical index in the unpadded tensor, or std::nullopt when qtt_idx addresses a zero-padded element (any reconstructed per-dimension index ≥ grid_sizes[d]). std::out_of_range if qtt_idx >= product(padded_grid_sizes). std::invalid_argument if the descriptor is empty or inconsistent.

### themis::tensor::TensorButterflyOperator

#### `TensorButterflyOperator(ButterflyConfig cfg)`
- Source: `include/tensor/tensor_butterfly_operator.h`:203
- Brief: n/a
- Parameters:
  - `cfg` (ButterflyConfig): n/a

#### `storage::TTTrain apply(const storage::TTTrain &data) const`
- Source: `include/tensor/tensor_butterfly_operator.h`:137
- Brief: Apply the operator to a TT-format data tensor.
- Parameters:
  - `data` (const storage::TTTrain &): Input TTTrain. data.mode_sizes must match grid_shape used in build().
- Return: Transformed TTTrain with the same shape as data.
- Throws:
  - std::invalid_argument: If data shape is incompatible with the operator.
  - std::logic_error: If operator type is not yet implemented.
- Details: Performs a mode-wise butterfly transform: for each mode k the n_k-length fibers of core G_k are transformed by the corresponding butterfly matrix. Complexity O(n · d · log₂(n) · r²) where n = max mode size, d = order, r = max rank. Reported as O(n·d) when n and r are treated as fixed constants (typical for structured compression workloads where r ≤ 16 and n ≤ 256 per mode). data Input TTTrain. data.mode_sizes must match grid_shape used in build(). Transformed TTTrain with the same shape as data. std::invalid_argument If data shape is incompatible with the operator. std::logic_error If operator type is not yet implemented.

#### `TensorButterflyOperator build(OperatorType type, const std::vector< std::size_t > &grid_shape, float precision=1e-6f)`
- Source: `include/tensor/tensor_butterfly_operator.h`:112
- Brief: Build the butterfly operator for the requested type and grid shape.
- Parameters:
  - `type` (OperatorType): Operator family (FOURIER, RADON, GREENS_FUNCTION).
  - `grid_shape` (const std::vector< std::size_t > &): Mode sizes matching the target TTTrain (must be powers of 2 for FOURIER).
  - `precision` (float): Relative reconstruction error tolerance.
- Throws:
  - std::invalid_argument: If grid_shape is empty or any mode size is not a positive power of 2 (FOURIER).
  - std::logic_error: If type is RADON or GREENS_FUNCTION (not yet implemented — STUB #171).
- Details: type Operator family (FOURIER, RADON, GREENS_FUNCTION). grid_shape Mode sizes matching the target TTTrain (must be powers of 2 for FOURIER). precision Relative reconstruction error tolerance. std::invalid_argument If grid_shape is empty or any mode size is not a positive power of 2 (FOURIER). std::logic_error If type is RADON or GREENS_FUNCTION (not yet implemented — STUB #171).

#### `std::vector< SimilarityResult > findSimilar(const std::string &query_key, std::size_t k) const`
- Source: `include/tensor/tensor_fingerprint_graph.h`:145
- Brief: Find the top-k adapters most similar to the query adapter.
- Parameters:
  - `query_key` (const std::string &): Storage key of the query adapter (must be registered).
  - `k` (std::size_t): Maximum number of results to return.
- Return: Sorted list (descending score) of similar adapters. Empty if query_key is not registered or k == 0.
- Details: Similarity is computed as cosine similarity in the TT domain using TensorTrainDecomposer::innerProduct(). The query adapter itself is excluded from the result list. query_key Storage key of the query adapter (must be registered). k Maximum number of results to return. Sorted list (descending score) of similar adapters. Empty if query_key is not registered or k == 0. Complexity: O(N * C_ip) for N candidates and TT inner-product cost C_ip. With setExactSimilarityFn(), complexity becomes O(N * C_backend).

- Details: Useful when the caller holds a fingerprint not yet stored in the graph. This path is intentionally approximate and does not use TT inner-product. fingerprint Query fingerprint vector. k Maximum number of results to return. tenant_id If non-empty, restrict results to this tenant. Complexity: O(N * D_overlap) where D_overlap is the shared prefix length of compared fingerprints. Missing dimensions are treated as zeros.

#### `std::unique_ptr< TensorMmapBridge > mapCores(const std::string &tenant_id, const std::string &collection, const std::string &field, int64_t id) const`
- Source: `include/tensor/tensor_index_manager.h`:179
- Brief: Pin TT-core pages in RAM and return an RAII bridge object.
- Parameters:
  - `tenant_id` (const std::string &): Tenant namespace.
  - `collection` (const std::string &): Collection name.
  - `field` (const std::string &): Field name.
  - `id` (int64_t): Vector ID.
- Return: Owning bridge; nullptr if the vector does not exist.
- Details: Each TT-core for the requested vector is placed into a dedicated mmap(MAP_ANONYMOUS\|MAP_PRIVATE) region and locked via mlock(). The returned TensorMmapBridge owns all regions; its destructor calls munlock() + munmap() automatically. Clients should store the bridge object alive as long as any pointer from bridge->slices() is being used (e.g. by a GGML graph node). tenant_id Tenant namespace. collection Collection name. field Field name. id Vector ID. Owning bridge; nullptr if the vector does not exist. STUB #176 — currently uses MAP_ANONYMOUS + memcpy. Real zero-copy (MAP_SHARED on RocksDB SST pages) is deferred to Q1 2027 (see src/tensor/FUTURE_ENHANCEMENTS.md).

- Source: `include/tensor/tensor_ingestion_bridge.h`:110
- Brief: κ-gate: returns true when estimated compression ratio ≥ min_kappa.
- Parameters:
  - `embedding` (const std::vector< float > &): n/a
  - `min_kappa` (double): n/a
- Details: Runs a pilot TT-decomposition with a coarser tolerance (pilot_eps = epsilon / 5, min 0.05) to quickly estimate the achievable compression ratio without the full cost. For embeddings with dim ≤ 1024, the full embedding is used for the pilot. For dim > 1024, a random projection to 1024 elements is used to keep the pilot cost bounded.

#### `std::unique_ptr< TensorMmapBridge > buildFromFd(const storage::TTTrain &train, int fd, std::size_t byte_offset=0)`
- Source: `include/tensor/tensor_mmap_bridge.h`:168
- Brief: Build a bridge using MAP_SHARED on a backing file descriptor.
- Parameters:
  - `train` (const storage::TTTrain &): Decomposed TT-train whose float data populates the map.
  - `fd` (int): Open read-only file descriptor for the backing SST file. Pass -1 to always use the MAP_ANONYMOUS fallback.
  - `byte_offset` (std::size_t): Byte offset inside fd where the TT data starts.
- Details: When fd is a valid open file descriptor (≥ 0) and the platform supports mmap(MAP_SHARED), each TT-core region is mapped directly from the file at the given byte_offset without any memcpy. This is the production zero-copy path for SST-resident TT-trains. Ownership: the caller retains ownership of fd; the bridge does not close it. Regions mapped with MAP_SHARED are tracked as externally_owned = true (the destructor calls munmap but NOT close). PERMANENT FALLBACK NOTE: When fd < 0, or when the platform does not support mmap(MAP_SHARED), the call transparently falls back to buildFromTrain(train) (MAP_ANONYMOUS memcpy). train Decomposed TT-train whose float data populates the map. fd Open read-only file descriptor for the backing SST file. Pass -1 to always use the MAP_ANONYMOUS fallback. byte_offset Byte offset inside fd where the TT data starts.

#### `std::unique_ptr< TensorMmapBridge > buildFromTrain(const storage::TTTrain &train)`
- Source: `include/tensor/tensor_mmap_bridge.h`:143
- Brief: Build a bridge from a TTTrain using MAP_ANONYMOUS + memcpy.
- Parameters:
  - `train` (const storage::TTTrain &): n/a
- Details: Page-pins each TT-core array in an anonymous mmap region so that the GGML bridge can reference data pointers without holding the index lock. When an SST-map function is injected via setSstMapFn(), the zero-copy MAP_SHARED path is used for any core for which the function returns a non-null pointer. PERMANENT FALLBACK NOTE: This is the always-available fallback path. For zero-copy MAP_SHARED over real SST file pages, inject a SstMapFn via setSstMapFn() or use buildFromFd() when a backing file descriptor is available.

#### `tensor::HTTrain fromDocument(const std::string &text, DocumentStructureHint hint=DocumentStructureHint::PARAGRAPHS, const UTRConfig &cfg={})`
- Source: `include/tensor/utr_converter.h`:295
- Brief: Encode a document as a hierarchical TT (HTTrain).

  - `cfg` (const UTRConfig &): Input parameter.

  - std::invalid_argument: if an error occurs.
  - std::runtime_error: if an error occurs.
- Details: ============================================================================ UTRConverter::fromDocument ============================================================================ The document is split into segments (paragraphs or sentences depending on hint). Each segment is encoded as a fixed-length embedding vector. The encoding tier is selected by priority: Registered ITextEncoder (if isAvailable()) Injected EmbedFn bridge (STUB #257) Built-in lexical encoder (unigram + bigram + char-trigram features, L2-normalised) — degraded mode, EncoderQuality::LEXICAL The resulting segment × embed_dim matrix is decomposed via HT-SVD into an HTTrain. text Raw document text (UTF-8). hint Segmentation strategy. cfg UTR configuration. HTTrain encoding the hierarchical document structure. std::invalid_argument if text is empty or produces no segments. std::runtime_error if the active EmbedFn or ITextEncoder returns a vector of the wrong size (fail-closed on mismatch). text Input parameter. hint Input parameter. cfg Input parameter. Return value. std::invalid_argument if an error occurs. std::runtime_error if an error occurs. Calls: empty(), splitSentences(), splitParagraphs(), size(), resize(), lk(), isAvailable(), reserve().

If a learned IImageEncoder is registered and isAvailable(), it is used in preference to the built-in patch-statistics encoder. pixels Flat HWC float values (length = h × w × c). h Image height in pixels. w Image width in pixels. c Number of channels (1 = grayscale, 3 = RGB, etc.) cfg UTR configuration. - Brief: Encode tabular data as a HyperIndexTensor (TT-encoded co-occurrence).

  - `tenant_id` (const std::string &): Identifier of the tenant.
  - `schema` (const std::vector< ColumnSchema > &): Input parameter.
  - `rows` (const std::vector< TableRow > &): Input parameter.
  - `cfg` (const UTRConfig &): Input parameter.
- Return: HyperIndexTensor encoding cross-column relationships.
- Throws:
  - std::invalid_argument: if schema has < 2 columns or rows is empty.
- Details: ============================================================================ UTRConverter::fromTabular ============================================================================ Delegates to HyperIndexBuilder::fromSchema(). tenant_id Owning tenant. schema Column descriptors. rows Data rows. cfg UTR configuration. HyperIndexTensor encoding cross-column relationships. std::invalid_argument if schema has < 2 columns or rows is empty. tenant_id Identifier of the tenant. schema Input parameter. rows Input parameter. cfg Input parameter. Return value. Calls: HyperIndexBuilder::fromSchema().

#### `void setImageEmbedFn(ImageEmbedFn fn)`
- Source: `include/tensor/utr_converter.h`:156
- Brief: Replace the raw-pixel TT encoding with a real patch-embedding backend.

#### `void setImageEncoder(std::shared_ptr< IImageEncoder > encoder)`
- Source: `include/tensor/utr_converter.h`:202
- Brief: Register a learned or high-quality image encoder.
- Parameters:
  - `encoder` (std::shared_ptr< IImageEncoder >): Input parameter.
- Details: Set Image Encoder. The encoder replaces the built-in patch-statistics fallback in fromImage(). If encoder->isAvailable() returns false at call time, the system falls back to the ImageEmbedFn bridge (if set) or the built-in patch-statistics encoder. Pass nullptr to clear any previously registered encoder. encoder Shared pointer to an IImageEncoder implementation, or nullptr to clear. encoder Input parameter. Calls: lk(), std::move().

#### `void setTextEncoder(std::shared_ptr< ITextEncoder > encoder)`
- Source: `include/tensor/utr_converter.h`:180
- Brief: Register a learned or high-quality text encoder.
- Parameters:
  - `encoder` (std::shared_ptr< ITextEncoder >): Input parameter.
- Details: ============================================================================ Encoder object registration — ITextEncoder / IImageEncoder ============================================================================ The encoder replaces the built-in lexical fallback in fromDocument(). If encoder->isAvailable() returns false at call time, the system falls back to the EmbedFn bridge (if set) or the built-in lexical encoder. Pass nullptr to clear any previously registered encoder. encoder Shared pointer to an ITextEncoder implementation, or nullptr to clear. encoder Input parameter. Calls: lk(), std::move().
